Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the sr object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 578. The moduli space has dimension at most 204 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on sr structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 578. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 204. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the sr structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 72. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 923 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the sr space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 72 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 923. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
